- **strategy_mean_reversion**: This skill should be used when invoked by master_trading with the mean_reversion strategy enabled, or when the user asks to "buy the laggard", "find the worst performer", "apply mean reversion". Cross-sectional, contrarian. Compares each buyable stock's N-day return against the pool's median return; the worst k laggards (those falling at least min_underperformance_percent below the median) get a small buy. Falling-knife guard via 50-day moving average; rebuy throttle via min_hours_between_buys on top of the 2-trading-day H1B floor. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_mean_reversion) · ★4)
- **strategy_mean_reversion_v2**: Internal sub-skill of master_trading_v2; called mechanically by tick.sh during Phase B. Same logic as v1 strategy_mean_reversion (cross-sectional contrarian, bottom-k laggards, 50-day MA falling-knife guard, rebuy throttle). Operators don't normally invoke this directly — use /master_trading_v2. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_mean_reversion_v2) · ★4)
- **strategy_profit_take**: This skill should be used when invoked by master_trading with the profit_take strategy enabled, or when the user asks to "scale out", "take profits", "lock in gains", "tier-out my position". Eager partial-exit on absolute gain. For each stock in the sellable set, sells a fraction of the original (baseline) position when current gain hits each threshold in a configured ladder. Pairs with trailing_stop — profit_take captures gains as they happen on the way up; trailing_stop captures the residual position after a real reversal.
